/**
Cart Page
**/

@media (min-width: 992px) {
	.elementor-widget-woocommerce-cart .e-cart__container {
		grid-template-columns: 50% auto;
	}	
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
    .elementor-widget-woocommerce-cart .e-cart__container {
        grid-template-areas: 
            "second"
            "first";
    }

    .elementor-widget-woocommerce-cart .e-cart__container > *:first-child {
        grid-area: first;
    }

    .elementor-widget-woocommerce-cart .e-cart__container > *:last-child {
        grid-area: second;
    }
}

#user-registration .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form-login .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid>div:not(#ur-recaptcha-node) .user-registration-Button {
	display: flex;
	margin: 0 auto;
	width: max-content;
}

<style type="text/css">
/******************************************
** USER REGISTRATION &  MEMBERSHIP STYLES **
*******************************************/

#user-registration,
.user-registration.ur-frontend-form {
	background: #FAFAFA;
}

body:not(.woocommerce-cart).user-registration-page #user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form--flat {
	background: #FAFAFA;
}

body.user-registration-page #user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount):has(.ur-frontend-form--flat), 
body.user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount):has(.ur-frontend-form--flat) {
	border: none;
}

#user-registration {
	margin: 0;
}

@media (min-width: 992px) {
	body:not(.woocommerce-cart) #user-registration .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form-login .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > div:not(#ur-recaptcha-node),
	body:not(.woocommerce-cart).user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) {
		width: 500px;	
	}
}

.ur-frontend-form.login,
.user-registration.ur-frontend-form {
	padding: 0;
}

body.user-registration-page #user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > div .user-registration-Button, 
body.user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > div .user-registration-Button,
body.user-registration-page .user-registration.ur-frontend-form--flat:not(.login) form .ur-button-container .ur-submit-button,
#user-registration.vertical .user-registration-MyAccount-content__body .ur-frontend-form.login .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > p .user-registration-Button.button,
.ur-frontend-form.login .ur-reset-password-btn {
	background: #f6db7b;
	font-size: 18px;
	border-radius: 90px;
	padding: 22px 45px;
	color: #000000;
	position: relative;
	border: none;
  	box-shadow: none;
	font-weight: 600;
}

.woocommerce-form .woocommerce-button[type="submit"],
.woocommerce-ResetPassword .woocommerce-Button[type="submit"],
.woocommerce-MyAccount-content .woocommerce-Button[type="submit"],
.user-registration-ResetPassword input[type="submit"] {
	background: #f6db7b;
	font-size: 18px;
	border-radius: 90px;
	padding: 18px 45px;
	color: #000000;
	position: relative;
}

.user-registration .button-primary {
	background: #f6db7b !important;
	border-radius: 90px !important;
	padding: 18px 45px !important;
	color: #000000 !important;
	border: none !important;
}

.user-registration .button-primary:hover {
	background: #F5CF72 !important;
}

.ur-frontend-form div[data-field="login-button"] {
	max-width: 350px;
  margin: 28px auto 0;
}

body.user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-button-container {
	margin: 32px auto 0;
}

body.user-registration-page #user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > div .user-registration-Button:hover, 
body.user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid > div .user-registration-Button:hover,
.ur-frontend-form.login .ur-reset-password-btn:hover {
	background: #F5CF72;
	color: #000000;
}

body.user-registration-page .user-registration.ur-frontend-form--flat:not(.login) form .ur-button-container .ur-submit-button:hover {
	background: #F5CF72;
}

.ur-frontend-form.ur-frontend-form--flat form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid label {
	color: #000000;
}

.user-registration-ResetPassword input[type="submit"] {
	border: none !important;
	border-radius: 90px !important;
}

body.user-registration-page #user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid .user-registration-form-row .input-wrapper input,
body.user-registration-page .user-registration:not(.user-registration-MyAccount) .ur-frontend-form .user-registration-form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid .user-registration-form-row .input-wrapper input,
body.user-registration-page .user-registration.ur-frontend-form--flat:not(.login) form .ur-form-row .ur-form-grid input:not([type=radio]),

<style type="text/css">
/* Shoptimizer Cart */

.shoptimizer-cart .amount {
    display: none;
}

.shoptimizer-cart-icon svg {
    display: none;
}

.shoptimizer-cart-icon {
    background-image: url('https://primepeptides.co/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/shopping-bag-dark.svg');
    background-size: 24px 24px;
    background-position: left center;
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    padding-left: 32px;
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
}

.shoptimizer-cart a.cart-contents .shoptimizer-cart-icon .mini-count {
    background-color: unset !important;
    border: none;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    font-size: 18px;
    color: currentcolor;
}

.shoptimizer-cart a.cart-contents:hover .shoptimizer-cart-icon .mini-count {
    top: 0;
}

/* End Shoptimizer Cart */

.elementor-nav-menu--main ul li > a:hover {
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.elementor-menu-cart--items-indicator-plain .elementor-menu-cart__toggle .elementor-button-icon {
    flex-direction: row-reverse;
}

.elementor-menu-cart__toggle .elementor-button svg {
    fill: none;
}

.elementor-menu-cart--items-indicator-plain .elementor-menu-cart__toggle .elementor-button-icon .elementor-button-icon-qty[data-counter] {
    font-size: 18px;
}

.elementor-menu-cart__toggle .elementor-button:hover .elementor-button-icon {
    color: #fff;
}

.elementor-menu-cart__toggle .elementor-button:hover svg {
    fill: none;
}

/*Transparent Header -- Light / Default*/

.header-quick-links svg {
    fill: white;
}

.header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
    filter: invert(1);
    color: #000;
    padding-left: 46px;
}

.header-quick-links .mini-count::before {
    width: 1px;
    height: 100%;
    content: '';
    background: #909090;
    position: absolute;
    left: -10px;
}

#page:has(.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active)::after {
    content: '';
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    background: #000000a6;
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
}

body:has(#page .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active) {
    height: 100vh;
    overflow: hidden;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .main-header-container {
    background: #FAFAFA;
    box-shadow: 4px 4px 40px 0 #0000001A;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .main-header-container:has(.elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active) {
    border-radius: 35px 35px 0px 0px;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects svg {
    fill: #000 !important;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-nav-menu .elementor-item {
    color: #000 !important;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
    filter: none;
}

.header-style-transparent_light .main-header .user-icon svg {
    fill: none !important;
}

.header-style-transparent_light .main-header .user-icon svg path {
    stroke: #fff;
}

.header-style-transparent_light .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .user-icon svg path {
    stroke: #000;
}

/* transparent LIGHT  */

body:not(.header-style-internal_dark):not(.header-style-transparent_dark) .elementor-43349 .elementor-element.elementor-element-54866f0.elementor-view-default .elementor-icon svg .cls-2 {
	fill: #fff;
}

body:not(.header-style-internal_dark):not(.header-style-transparent_dark) .elementor-43349 .elementor-element.elementor-element-54866f0.elementor-view-default .elementor-icon svg .r-circle {
	stroke: #fff;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects svg .cls-2 {
	fill: #000 !important;
}

.main-header.elementor-sticky--effects svg .r-circle {
	stroke: #000 !important;
}

/*Transparent Header dark*/
.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header-menu {
    background-color: #00000010 !important;
    border-color: transparent !important;
}

.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header svg {
    fill: #000 !important;
}

.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
    filter: none;
}

.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .elementor-item {
    color: #000000 !important;
}


.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .user-icon svg {
    fill: none !important;
}

.header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .user-icon svg path {
    stroke: #000;
}


/*Internal -- Dark */


.header-style-internal_dark .main-header-container {
    background: #FAFAFA;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects {
    background: transparent;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header-menu {
    background-color: #00000010 !important;
    border-color: transparent !important;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .main-header-menu {
    background-color: transparent !important;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header svg {
    fill: #000 !important;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header .header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
    filter: none;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .elementor-nav-menu--main .elementor-item {
   color: #000 !important;
}


.header-style-internal_dark .main-header .user-icon svg {
    fill: none !important;
}

.header-style-internal_dark .main-header .user-icon svg path {
    stroke: #000;
}

@media (max-width: 1024px) {
	.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle:hover {
		border-width: 0 !important;
	}
}

@media (min-width: 768px) and (max-width: 1024px) {

    .main-header .header-quick-links .mini-count::before {
        display: none;
    }

    .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-nav-menu .elementor-item,
    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .elementor-item {
        color: #fff !important;
    }

    ul#menu-2-d48b2c7 li:last-child a {
        border-bottom-right-radius: 30px;
        border-bottom-left-radius: 30px;
    }

    .main-header .shoptimizer-cart a.cart-contents .shoptimizer-cart-icon .mini-count {
        width: 0;
        opacity: 0;
    }

    .main-header .header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
        padding: 0px 15px;
        top: 0px;
    }

    .main-header .main-header-menu {
	    min-width: 45px;
    	height: 45px;
    	padding: 0 !important;
		border: 1px solid #00000020 !important;
	}

    .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle {
        width: 100%;
		height: 100%;
		padding: 0;
		border: 0;
		background-color: transparent;
    }
	
	.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle__icon--open, .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active {
		width: 24px;
		height: 100%;
		background-color: transparent!important;
	}
	
	.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle svg{
		width: 100%;
		height: 100%;
	}

    .header-style-transparent_light .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle svg path {
        fill: #fff;
    }

    .header-style-transparent_light .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-menu-toggle svg path {
        fill: #000;
    }

    .main-header .main-header-container {
        padding-right: 1rem;
        padding-left: 1rem;
    }

    .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .main-header-menu {
        border-color: #00000020 !important;
		background-color: #00000010!important
    }

    .header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .main-header-menu {
        border-color: #00000020 !important;
    }

    .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-nav-menu__container {
        background-color: transparent !important;
        transition: max-height .3s, transform .3s;
        animation: ease !important;
    }

    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .elementor-nav-menu__container {
        background-color: transparent !important;
        transition: max-height .3s, transform .3s;
        animation: ease !important;
    }

    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .main-header-container:has(.elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active) {
        border-radius: 35px 35px 0px 0px;
    }

    .main-header .elementor-nav-menu {
        width: calc(100% - 2rem);
        background-color: #010C33;
        border-radius: 0 0 30px 30px;
    }

    .main-header .main-header-logo {
        margin-right: auto;
    }

}

@media (max-width: 767px) and (min-width: 320px) {

    .main-header .header-quick-links .mini-count::before {
        display: none;
    }

    .main-header .shoptimizer-cart a.cart-contents .shoptimizer-cart-icon .mini-count {
        width: 0;
        opacity: 0;
    }

    .main-header .header-quick-links .shoptimizer-cart-icon {
        padding: 0px 15px;
        top: 0px;
    }

    ul#menu-2-d48b2c7 li:last-child a {

        border-bottom-left-radius: 30px;
    }

    .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-nav-menu .elementor-item,
    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .elementor-item {
        color: #fff !important;
    }

    .main-header .main-header-menu {
	    min-width: 45px;
    	height: 45px;
    	padding: 0 !important;
		border: 1px solid #00000020 !important;
		background-color: #00000010 !important;
	}

    .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle {
        width: 100%;
		height: 100%;	
		background-color: transparent;
    }
	
	.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle__icon--open {
		width: 24px;
		height: 24px;
	}
	
	.main-header .elementor-menu-toggle__icon--open svg{
		width: 100%;
		height: 100%;
	}

    .header-style-transparent_light .main-header .elementor-menu-toggle svg path {
        fill: #fff;
    }

    .header-style-transparent_light .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-menu-toggle svg path {
        fill: #000;
    }

    .main-header .main-header-container {
        padding-right: 1rem;
        padding-left: 1rem;
    }

/*     .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .main-header-menu {
        border-color: #00000010 !important;
    } */

/*     .header-style-transparent_dark .main-header .main-header-menu {
        border-color: #00000010 !important;
    }
 */
    .main-header.elementor-sticky--effects .elementor-nav-menu__container {
        background-color: transparent !important;
        transition: max-height .3s, transform .3s;
        animation: ease !important;
    }

    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .elementor-nav-menu__container {
        background-color: transparent !important;
        transition: max-height .3s, transform .3s;
        animation: ease !important;
    }

    .header-style-internal_dark .main-header .main-header-container:has(.elementor-menu-toggle.elementor-active) {
        border-radius: 35px 35px 0px 0px;
    }

    .main-header .elementor-nav-menu {

<style type="text/css">
.global-button-main svg {
    font-size: 56px;
}
.product p.stock {
	display: block !important;
	padding: 6px 10px !important;
	background: var( --e-global-color-primary ) !important;
	border-radius: 100px;
}
/* .product p.stock.in-stock, */
.product .card-stock p.stock.in-stock {
	display: none !important;
}
.single-product p.stock {
    display: block !important;
    font-size: 16px;
    margin-bottom: 16px;
	background: transparent !important;
	border-radius: 0px !important;
}
.global-button-main .elementor-button-text {
    align-content: center;
}

#calculator-div .other-field,
#calculator-div .other-field1,
#calculator-div .other-field2,
#vialCustomInput,
#doseCustomInput {
    display: none;
}

#calculator-div .scale-color {
    height: 0px;
}

.calculator-list ul .elementor-icon-list-text {
    padding: 16px 12px;
    border: 1px solid #00000010;
    border-radius: 16px;
    margin-bottom: 16px;
}

#vialButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button,
#doseButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button {
    width: 120px !important;
    display: flex;
    border: 1.5px solid #dedada;
    padding: 12px;
    margin-bottom: 15px;
    cursor: pointer;
    text-align: center;
    align-items: center !important;
    border-radius: 16px;
    justify-content: center;
}

#vialButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button:hover,
#doseButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button:hover {
    border: 1.5px solid #000;
}

#vialButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button.selected,
#doseButtons ul .elementor-icon-list-text .select-button.selected {
    border: none !important;
    outline: none;
}

.shop-peptide .elementor-swiper-button-prev,
.shop-peptide .elementor-swiper-button-next {
    background-color: #F6DB7B;
    padding: 15px;
    border-radius: 40px;
}

.dialog-message.dialog-lightbox-message,
.dialog-widget-content.dialog-lightbox-widget-content.animated {
    border-radius: 20px;
}

/* css for posts button starts */

.posts_navigation .global-button-main.elementor-align-right .elementor-button {
	background-color: #F6DB7B;
}

.posts_navigation .global-button-main.elementor-align-right .elementor-button:hover {
	background-color: #F5CF72;
}

.posts_navigation .elementor-button {
    font-size: 18px;
	color: #000;
	border-radius: 99px;
}

.posts_navigation .posts-nav-next {
	padding: 9.5px 9.5px 9.5px 32px;
}

.posts_navigation .posts-nav-prev {
	padding: 26.5px 32px 26.5px;
	background-color: transparent;
	border: 1px solid #00000010;
}

.posts_navigation .elementor-button:hover {
    background-color: #f6db7b;
}

.posts_navigation {
    display: flex;
    width: 100%;
    flex-direction: row !important;
    justify-content: space-between;
}

.posts_navigation .global-button-main.elementor-align-left svg {
    rotate: 200deg;
}

.posts_navigation .elementor-button-content-wrapper {
    gap: 32px;
}

/* css for posts button ends */


#elementor-popup-modal-43467 .dialog-close-button svg {
    fill: #fff;
}

#vialCustomInput input,
#doseCustomInput input {
    border: 1px solid #000;
    width: 120px;
}

#vialButtons ul,
#doseButtons ul {
    justify-content: left;
}

.calculator-list ul {
    justify-content: space-between;
}

.peptide-accordion .e-n-accordion-item-title-header:before,
.calculating-accordion .e-n-accordion-item-title-header:before {
    display: none;
}

.calculator-list .elementor-icon-list-text.active,
#bac-calculator .selected {
    background-color: #F6DB7B;
}

.calculator-list .elementor-icon-list-text:hover {
    border: 1px solid #0e1b4d75;
}

.calculator-list.ul-calculator .elementor-icon-list-text:hover {
    border: none;
}

.calculator-list .elementor-icon-list-text {
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 5s ease;

}

.calculating-accordion .count {
    padding: 10px 20px;
    background-color: #F6DB7B;
    border-radius: 40px;
    margin-right: 16px;
}

.peptide-accordion details {
    border: 1px solid #EDEDED;
    border-radius: 40px;
    overflow: hidden;
    border-bottom-color: #EDEDED !important;
    transition: padding 0.5s ease, border-color 0.3s ease;
    background-color: #fff;
}

.blog-text-widget h2 {
    font-size: 56px;
    font-family: "Roobert Pro", sans-serif;
}

.blog-text-widget h3 {
    font-size: 32px;
    font-family: "Roobert Pro", sans-serif;
    font-weight: 500;
    line-height: 36px;
}

.main-container {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 80px;
}

.calculator-section .elementor-widget.elementor-align-start .elementor-inline-items {
    justify-content: space-between;
}

.blog-text-widget a {
    text-decoration: underline;
}

.calculator-section .elementor-icon-list-text {
    font-family: "Archivo", Sans-serif;
    font-size: 14px;
    font-weight: 600;
    width: 75px;
    text-align: center;
}

/* carousel css */

#reconstituation-carousel {
    position: relative;
}

#reconstituation-carousel .swiper-pagination {
    top: -11%;
    left: 85%;
}

#reconstituation-carousel .swiper-pagination,
#reconstituation-carousel .elementor-swiper-button-prev,
#reconstituation-carousel .elementor-swiper-button-next {
    position: absolute;
}

.section-slider {
    overflow-x: hidden;
}

/* css for search bar */

.dgwt-wcas-darkened-overlay>div {
    overflow: hidden;
}

.dgwt-wcas-has-headings.js-dgwt-wcas-initialized {
    z-index: 13 !important;
    width: 50vw !important;
    left: 25% !important;
}

input#dgwt-wcas-search-input-1 {
    width: 50vw;
    padding: 13px 24px 13px 24px;
}

.dgwt-wcas-style-pirx .dgwt-wcas-sf-wrapp {
    padding: 10px;
    height: 70px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
}

.dgwt-wcas-suggestions-wrapp-fixed {
    z-index: 1 !important;
}

button.dgwt-wcas-search-submit {
    position: relative !important;
    top: 0px !important;
    left: -45px !important;
}

.dgwt-wcas-sf-wrapp {
    position: fixed;
    left: 0;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 999;
    width: 100vw;
    place-content: center;
}

.dgwt-wcas-overlay-mobile {
    background: rgba(1, 12, 51, .5);
}

.dgwt-wcas-sf-wrapp .dgwt-wcas-close {
    left: 75%;
}

.dgwt-wcas-open .dgwt-wcas-sf-wrapp {
    z-index: 15 !important;
}

.dgwt-wcas-darkened-overlay>div {
    background: transparent!important;
}

body.dgwt-wcas-darkoverl-on .main-header .dgwt-wcas-sf-wrapp::before {
    background: #0000008a;
    position: fixed;
    content: '';
    width: 100%;
    height: 100%;
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
    top: 0;
    right: 0;
    z-index: -1;
}

body.dgwt-wcas-focused .mobile-menu.close-drawer {
    display: block;
    position: fixed;
    top: 23px;
    right: 2px;
    background: #000;
    color: #fff;
    height: 25px;
    width: 25px;
    z-index: 12;
    border-radius: 50%;
    padding: 4px;
}

.dgwt-wcas-open .main-header {
    z-index: 1;
} */

/* css for share tool tip	 */
/* css for share tool tip	 */
/* css for share tool tip	 */

/* Tooltip container */
.btn-share {
    position: relative;
    display: inline-block;
    cursor: pointer;
}

/* Tooltip text */
.btn-share::after {
    content: attr(data-tooltip);
    position: absolute;
    top: -30px;
    /* above the button */
    left: 50%;
    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(5px);
    background: var(--e-global-color-primary);
    font-family: "Archivo", Sans-serif;
    color: var(--e-global-color-text);
    font-size: 12px;
    padding: 4px 8px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    white-space: nowrap;
    transition: opacity 0.2s ease, transform 0.2s ease;
    z-index: 9999;
}

/* Show tooltip when copied */
.btn-share.copied::after {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
}

:is(.elementor-widget-woocommerce-product-add-to-cart,.woocommerce div.product .elementor-widget-woocommerce-product-add-to-cart,.elementor-widget-wc-add-to-cart,.woocommerce div.product .elementor-widget-wc-add-to-cart) form.cart .button:where(:not(:first-child)), :is(.elementor-widget-woocommerce-product-add-to-cart,.woocommerce div.product .elementor-widget-woocommerce-product-add-to-cart,.elementor-widget-wc-add-to-cart,.woocommerce div.product .elementor-widget-wc-add-to-cart) form.cart button:where(:not(:first-child)) {
	margin-block-start: 20px;
}

<!-- start Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<script type="text/javascript">
(function() {
  const speedFactor = 1.5;

  function initProgress() {
    const section = document.getElementById('section-progress');
    if (!section) return;
    const fill = section.querySelector('.progress-fill');
    if (!fill) return;

    function update() {
      const rect = section.getBoundingClientRect();
      const vh = window.innerHeight;
      const sectionHeight = rect.height;

      if (rect.bottom <= 0) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) fill.style.height = '100%'; 
        else fill.style.width = '100%';
        return;
      }
      if (rect.top >= vh) {
        if (window.innerWidth > 767) fill.style.height = '0%';
        else fill.style.width = '0%';
        return;
      }

      const scrolled = Math.min(sectionHeight + vh, Math.max(0, vh - rect.top));
      const percent = Math.min(100, ((scrolled / (sectionHeight + vh)) * 100) * speedFactor);

      if (window.innerWidth > 767) {
        fill.style.height = percent + '%';
        fill.style.width = '';
      } else { // mobile
        fill.style.width = percent + '%';
        fill.style.height = '';
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('scroll', update);
    window.addEventListener('resize', update);
    update();
  }

  if (window.elementorFrontend) {
    elementorFrontend.on('frontend/init', initProgress);
  } else {
    window.addEventListener('load', initProgress);
  }
})();
</script>
<!-- end Simple Custom CSS and JS -->

					.e-con.e-parent:nth-of-type(n+2):not(.e-lazyloaded):not(.e-no-lazyload),
					.e-con.e-parent:nth-of-type(n+2):not(.e-lazyloaded):not(.e-no-lazyload) * {
						background-image: none !important;
					}
				}
			</style>
			<script>(function () {
  function updateKlarnaMainText() {
    const klarnaHost = document.querySelector('#osm_shadow_host');
    if (!klarnaHost || !klarnaHost.shadowRoot) return;

    const klarnaTextEl = klarnaHost.shadowRoot.querySelector('.osm_main_text');
    if (!klarnaTextEl) return;

    klarnaTextEl.innerHTML =
      '<strong>Accepted Cards via Klarna:</strong><br>' +
      'Visa, Discover, Maestro, Mastercard<br><br>' +
      '<strong>Not Accepted:</strong><br>' +
      'Chase, Capital One, American Express (AMEX)<br><br>' +
      '<strong>Tip:</strong><br>' +
      'If you have trouble at checkout, try a different card.';

    // Inject style once
    if (!klarnaHost.shadowRoot.querySelector('style[data-pp-klarna-style="1"]')) {
      const style = document.createElement('style');
      style.setAttribute('data-pp-klarna-style', '1');
      style.textContent = `
        #osm_container { max-height: unset !important; background: unset !important; }
        #osm_container > ul > li { align-items: unset !important; }
      `;
      klarnaHost.shadowRoot.appendChild(style);
    }
  }

  function initListeners() {
    // Only call if it exists (prevents fatal error)
    if (typeof removeCybersourceText === 'function') removeCybersourceText();

    updateKlarnaMainText();

    const checkoutForm = document.querySelector('form.checkout');
    if (checkoutForm && !checkoutForm.dataset.ppListenerAttached) {
      checkoutForm.dataset.ppListenerAttached = '1';
      checkoutForm.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
        if (e.target && e.target.name && e.target.name.indexOf('payment_method') === 0) {
          setTimeout(updateKlarnaMainText, 200);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  // Throttle observer callbacks
  let observerTimer = null;
  const observer = new MutationObserver(function () {
    if (observerTimer) return;
    observerTimer = setTimeout(function () {
      observerTimer = null;
      initListeners();
    }, 250);
  });

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    initListeners();
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  });
})();</script><link rel="icon" href="https://primepeptides.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/PP-Favicon-50x50.png" sizes="32x32" />
<link rel="icon" href="https://primepeptides.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/PP-Favicon-300x300.png" sizes="192x192" />
<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="https://primepeptides.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/PP-Favicon-300x300.png" />
<meta name="msapplication-TileImage" content="https://primepeptides.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/PP-Favicon-300x300.png" />

									<p>All of our products undergo rigorous third-party mass spectrometry testing with <a href="https://janoshik.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Janoshik Analytical</a>.</p>								</div>

									<p>Our peptides are lyophilized (freeze dried) for shelf stability during shipping and handling, to ensure they arrive to you with maximally potent. They require bacteriostatic water for research use. Please view our FAQs on reconstitution for resources on mixing.</p>								</div>

				<div role="region" aria-labelledby="e-n-accordion-item-5163" class="elementor-element elementor-element-5d57690 e-flex e-con-boxed e-con e-child" data-id="5d57690" data-element_type="container" data-e-type="container">
					<div class="e-con-inner">
		<div role="region" aria-labelledby="e-n-accordion-item-5163" class="elementor-element elementor-element-5ace65c e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child" data-id="5ace65c" data-element_type="container" data-e-type="container">
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-de42e2d elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="de42e2d" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
									<p>We are happy to accept returns of unopened vials within a month of purchase. To initiate a return request or refund request, log in to your account using the same email address used at checkout. Navigate to the <a href="https://primepeptides.co/my-account" target="_blank" rel="noopener">My Account</a> page, select the <a href="https://primepeptides.co/my-account/orders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Orders</a> tab, and click Return Request next to the relevant order. Complete the return request form, and our team will respond within 1–2 business days. We will issue a full refund when we receive the return package and can restock the inventory.</p>								</div>
				</div>

				</div>

						<details id="e-n-accordion-item-5164" class="e-n-accordion-item" >

<input type="hidden" id="commercekit_nonce" name="commercekit_nonce" value="4eae355b3b" /><input type="hidden" name="_wp_http_referer" value="/" /><script type="text/javascript">
/* <![CDATA[ */
document.addEventListener( 'DOMContentLoaded', function() {
	var cgkit_nonce_ustate = 0;
	var cgkit_nonce_cvalue = Cookies.get( 'commercekit-nonce-value' );
	var cgkit_nonce_cstate = Cookies.get( 'commercekit-nonce-state' );
	var cgkit_user_switched = 0;
	if ( cgkit_nonce_cvalue == '' || cgkit_nonce_cstate != cgkit_nonce_ustate || cgkit_user_switched == 1 ) {
		var timestamp = new Date().getTime();
		fetch( commercekit_ajs.ajax_url + '=commercekit_get_nonce&v=' + timestamp, {
			method: 'GET',
		} ).then( response => response.json() ).then( json => {
			if ( json.status == 1 ) {
				var twohrs = new Date( new Date().getTime() + 120 * 60 * 1000 );
				if ( window.Cookiebot ) { /* Cookiebot compatible */
					if ( window.Cookiebot.consent.preferences || window.Cookiebot.consent.statistics || window.Cookiebot.consent.marketing ) {
						Cookies.set( 'commercekit-nonce-value', json.nonce, { expires: twohrs } );
						Cookies.set( 'commercekit-nonce-state', json.state, { expires: twohrs } );
					} else {
						Cookies.remove( 'commercekit-nonce-value' );
						Cookies.remove( 'commercekit-nonce-state' );
					}
				} else {
					Cookies.set( 'commercekit-nonce-value', json.nonce, { expires: twohrs } );
					Cookies.set( 'commercekit-nonce-state', json.state, { expires: twohrs } );
				}
				cgkit_nonce_ustate = json.state;
				commercekit_update_nonce( json.nonce );
			}
		} );
	} else {
		commercekit_update_nonce( cgkit_nonce_cvalue );
	}
} );
function commercekit_update_nonce( nonce ) {
	var nonce_input = document.querySelector( '#commercekit_nonce' );
	if ( nonce_input ) {
		nonce_input.value = nonce;
	} else {
		document.body.insertAdjacentHTML( 'beforeend', '<' + 'input type="hidden" id="commercekit_nonce" name="commercekit_nonce" value="' + nonce + '"' + '>' );
	}
	commercekit_ajs.ajax_nonce = 1;
	if ( typeof cgkit_update_order_bump_views == 'function' ) {
		cgkit_update_order_bump_views();
	}
}
/* ]]> */
</script>
	<script type="text/javascript">
/* <![CDATA[ */
var cgkit_wbmc = document.querySelector( '.wc-block-mini-cart' );
if ( cgkit_wbmc ) {
	var cgkitMCBCurrentRequest = null;
	var cgkitMCBCurrentCancel = null;
	function cgkitLoadMiniCartBlocks() {
		var cgkit_drawer = document.querySelector( '.wc-block-mini-cart__drawer' );
		if ( ! cgkit_drawer ) {
			return;
		}
		clearTimeout( cgkitMCBCurrentRequest );
		cgkitMCBCurrentRequest = setTimeout( function() {
			if ( cgkitMCBCurrentCancel ) {
				cgkitMCBCurrentCancel.abort();
			}
			cgkitMCBCurrentCancel = new AbortController();
			var timestamp = new Date().getTime();
			fetch( commercekit_ajs.ajax_url + '=commercekit_mini_cart_blocks&v=' + timestamp, {
				signal: cgkitMCBCurrentCancel.signal,
				method: 'GET',
			} ).then( response => response.json() ).then( json => {
				if ( json.success ) {
					var cgkit_table = document.querySelector( '.wc-block-mini-cart__drawer .wc-block-mini-cart__items' );
					var cgkit_before = document.querySelector( '#cgkit-before-mini-cart-blocks' );
					if ( cgkit_before ) {
						cgkit_before.innerHTML = json.data.before_cart;
					} else if ( cgkit_table ) {
						cgkit_table.insertAdjacentHTML( 'afterbegin', '<div id="cgkit-before-mini-cart-blocks">' + json.data.before_cart + '</div>' );
					}
					var cgkit_after = document.querySelector( '#cgkit-after-mini-cart-blocks' );
					if ( cgkit_after ) {
						cgkit_after.innerHTML = json.data.after_cart;
					} else if ( cgkit_table ) {
						cgkit_table.insertAdjacentHTML( 'beforeend', '<div id="cgkit-after-mini-cart-blocks">' + json.data.after_cart + '</div>' );
					}
				}
			} ).catch( function( e ) { } );
		}, 100 );
	}
	document.addEventListener( 'click', function( e ) {
		$this = e.target;
		$thisp = $this.closest( '.wc-block-mini-cart' );
		if ( $this.classList.contains( 'wc-block-mini-cart' ) || $thisp ) {
			setTimeout( function() {
				cgkitLoadMiniCartBlocks();
			}, 500 );
		}
	} );
	( function() {
		const originalFetch = window.fetch;
		window.fetch = async function(...args) {
			const response = await originalFetch(...args);
			if ( args[0].includes( '/wc/store/v1/batch' ) || args[0].includes( '/wc/store/v1/cart' ) ) {
				cgkitLoadMiniCartBlocks();
			}
			return response;
		};
	} )();
}
/* ]]> */
</script>

						<script>
				const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
				} );
			</script>

============================================================
PRIME PEPTIDES - RESEARCH DISCLAIMER POPUP - - Filip Kuzmanovski
============================================================
Add this entire code block to your site's footer scripts.
You can use plugins like:

This will show a "Research Use Only" disclaimer popup to 
first-time visitors and remember their acknowledgment for 30 days.
============================================================
-->

<!-- Prime Peptides Research Disclaimer Modal -->
<div id="pp-disclaimer" class="pp-disclaimer" aria-hidden="true">
    <div class="pp-disclaimer__backdrop" data-pp-close></div>
    <div class="pp-disclaimer__dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="pp-disclaimer-title">
        <div class="pp-disclaimer__card">
            <div class="pp-disclaimer__header">
                <span class="pp-disclaimer__badge">Research</span>
                <h2 id="pp-disclaimer-title" class="pp-disclaimer__title">Research Use Only</h2>
            </div>
            <div class="pp-disclaimer__body">
                <p>By using this site, you acknowledge that all products and information are provided for
                    <strong>research purposes only</strong> and are not intended for human consumption or medical use.
                </p>
                <p>You must be <strong>18 years of age or older</strong> to use this website.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="pp-disclaimer__actions">
                <button type="button" class="pp-disclaimer__btn" data-pp-accept>I Understand</button>
            </div>
            <button type="button" class="pp-disclaimer__close" aria-label="Close" data-pp-close>
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"
                    stroke-linecap="round">
                    <line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18"></line>
                    <line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18"></line>
                </svg>
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<style>
    /* Prime Peptides Research Disclaimer Styles */
    :root {
        --pp-primary: #F6DB7B;
        --pp-primary-light: #127d8c;
        --pp-accent: #F5CF72;
        --pp-white: #ffffff;
		--pp-black: #000000;
        --pp-gray-50: #f4f7fb;
        --pp-gray-600: #4b5563;
        --pp-gray-800: #1f2937;
        --pp-backdrop: rgba(10, 20, 30, 0.65);
        --pp-shadow: 0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
        --pp-radius: 16px;
        --pp-radius-full: 999px;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer {
        position: fixed;
        inset: 0;
        z-index: 999999;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 16px;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        transition: opacity 0.3s ease, visibility 0.3s ease;
        font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Oxygen, Ubuntu, sans-serif;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer.is-open {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__backdrop {
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        background: var(--pp-backdrop);
        backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
        -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
        cursor: pointer;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__dialog {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 440px;
        transform: translateY(20px) scale(0.95);
        transition: transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
    }

    .pp-disclaimer.is-open .pp-disclaimer__dialog {
        transform: translateY(0) scale(1);
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__card {
        position: relative;
        background: linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--pp-white), var(--pp-gray-50));
        border-radius: var(--pp-radius);
        box-shadow: var(--pp-shadow);
        padding: 28px 28px 24px;
        overflow: hidden;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__card::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        height: 3px;
        background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--pp-primary), var(--pp-accent));
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__header {
        margin-bottom: 18px;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__badge {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        padding: 6px 14px;
        font-size: 13px;
        font-weight: 500;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
        color: var(--pp-black);
        background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--pp-primary), var(--pp-accent));
        border-radius: var(--pp-radius-full);
        text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    h2.pp-disclaimer__title {
        margin-top: 10px;
        font-size: 32px;
        color: var(--pp-gray-800);
        line-height: 1.2;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__body {
        margin-bottom: 24px;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__body p {
        margin: 0 0 12px;
        font-size: 15px;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--pp-gray-600);
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__body p:last-child {
        margin-bottom: 0;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__body strong {
        color: var(--pp-gray-800);
        font-weight: 600;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__actions {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: flex-end;
    }

    button.pp-disclaimer__btn {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 14px 28px;
        font-size: 15px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--pp-black);
        background: var(--pp-primary);
        border: none;
        border-radius: var(--pp-radius-full);
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: all 0.2s ease;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(15, 78, 99, 0.35);
		outline: none;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__btn:hover {
        background: var(--pp-accent);
        transform: translateY(-1px);
        box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(15, 78, 99, 0.4);
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__btn:active {
        transform: translateY(0);
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__close {
        position: absolute;
        top: 14px;
        right: 14px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        width: 36px;
        height: 36px;
        padding: 0;
        color: var(--pp-gray-600);
        background: var(--pp-white);
        border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
        border-radius: 10px;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: all 0.2s ease;
    }

    .pp-disclaimer__close:hover {
        color: var(--pp-gray-800);
        background: var(--pp-gray-50);
        border-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
    }

    body.pp-disclaimer-open {
        overflow: hidden;
    }

    @media (max-width: 480px) {
        .pp-disclaimer {
            padding: 12px;
            align-items: flex-end;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__card {
            padding: 24px 20px 20px;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__header {
            flex-wrap: wrap;
            gap: 10px;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__title {
            font-size: 20px;
            width: 100%;
            order: 2;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__badge {
            order: 1;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__body p {
            font-size: 14px;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__btn {
            width: 100%;
            padding: 16px 24px;
        }

        .pp-disclaimer__actions {
            justify-content: stretch;
        }
    }

    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {

        .pp-disclaimer,
        .pp-disclaimer__dialog,
        .pp-disclaimer__btn,
        .pp-disclaimer__close {
            transition: none;
        }
    }
</style>

<script>
    (function () {
        'use strict';

        var STORAGE_KEY = 'pp_research_acknowledged';
        var EXPIRY_DAYS = 30;

        var modal = document.getElementById('pp-disclaimer');
        if (!modal) return;

        function hasAcknowledged() {
            try {
                var stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
                if (!stored) return false;
                var data = JSON.parse(stored);
                var expiry = new Date(data.expiry);
                if (expiry > new Date()) {
                    return true;
                } else {
                    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
                    return false;
                }
            } catch (e) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        function saveAcknowledgment() {
            try {
                var expiry = new Date();
                expiry.setDate(expiry.getDate() + EXPIRY_DAYS);
                localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
                    acknowledged: true,
                    expiry: expiry.toISOString(),
                    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
                }));
            } catch (e) { }
        }

        function openModal() {
            modal.classList.add('is-open');
            modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
            document.body.classList.add('pp-disclaimer-open');
            var acceptBtn = modal.querySelector('[data-pp-accept]');
            if (acceptBtn) setTimeout(function () { acceptBtn.focus(); }, 100);
            document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
        }

        function closeModal() {
            modal.classList.remove('is-open');
            modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            document.body.classList.remove('pp-disclaimer-open');
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
        }

        function handleKeyDown(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                saveAcknowledgment();
                closeModal();
            }
            if (e.key === 'Tab') {
                var focusable = modal.querySelectorAll('button');
                var first = focusable[0];
                var last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
                if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    last.focus();
                } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    first.focus();
                }
            }
        }

        var acceptBtns = modal.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-accept]');
        for (var i = 0; i < acceptBtns.length; i++) {
            acceptBtns[i].addEventListener('click', function () {
                saveAcknowledgment();
                closeModal();
            });
        }

        var closeBtns = modal.querySelectorAll('[data-pp-close]');
        for (var j = 0; j < closeBtns.length; j++) {
            closeBtns[j].addEventListener('click', function () {
                saveAcknowledgment();
                closeModal();
            });
        }

        if (!hasAcknowledged()) {
            setTimeout(openModal, 300);
        }
    })();

<script type="text/javascript" id="shoptimizer-main-js-after">
/* <![CDATA[ */
			
			document.querySelectorAll('.woocommerce-image__wrapper').forEach(carousel => {
		    const carouselInner = carousel.querySelector('.shoptimizer-plp-image-wrapper');
		    const dots = carousel.querySelectorAll('.shoptimizer-plp-carousel--dot');
		    const carouselContainer = carousel.querySelector('.shoptimizer-plp-carousel-container');

		    // Check if the carousel container exists
		    if (carouselContainer) {
		        function updateDots() {
		            const scrollLeft = carouselContainer.scrollLeft;
		            const viewportWidth = carouselContainer.clientWidth;
		            const index = Math.round(scrollLeft / viewportWidth);

		            dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
		                dot.classList.toggle('active', i === index);
		            });
		        }

		        let isScrolling;
		        carouselContainer.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
		            clearTimeout(isScrolling);
		            isScrolling = setTimeout(() => {
		                updateDots();
		            }, 50);
		        });

		        // Initialize the dots
		        updateDots();
		    } else {
		        console.warn('Carousel container not found:', carousel);
		    }
		});

		

				jQuery( document ).ready( function( $ ) {
					$( 'body' ).on( 'added_to_cart', function( event, fragments, cart_hash ) {
						if ( ! $( 'body' ).hasClass( 'elementor-editor-active' ) ) {
							$( 'body' ).addClass( 'drawer-open' );
							$( '#shoptimizerCartDrawer').focus();
						}
					} );				
				} );
				document.addEventListener( 'DOMContentLoaded', function() {
					document.addEventListener( 'click', function( event ) {
						var is_inner = event.target.closest( '.shoptimizer-mini-cart-wrap' );
						if ( ! event.target.classList.contains( 'shoptimizer-mini-cart-wrap' ) && ! is_inner ) {
							document.querySelector( 'body' ).classList.remove( 'drawer-open' );
						}
						var is_inner2 = event.target.closest( '.shoptimizer-cart' );
						if ( event.target.classList.contains( 'shoptimizer-cart' ) || is_inner2 ) {
							var is_header = event.target.closest( '.site-header-cart' );
							var is_shortcode = event.target.closest( '.shoptimizer-cart-shortcode' );
							if ( is_header || is_shortcode ) {
								event.preventDefault();
								document.querySelector( 'body' ).classList.toggle( 'drawer-open' );
								document.getElementById('shoptimizerCartDrawer').focus();
							}
						}
						if ( event.target.classList.contains( 'close-drawer' ) ) {
							document.querySelector( 'body' ).classList.remove( 'drawer-open' );
						}
					} );
				} );

				// Mini Cart Ajax state.
				document.addEventListener( 'DOMContentLoaded', function() {
				    document.querySelector( '#ajax-loading' ).style.display = 'none';
				} );

				;
				( function( $ ) {
					'use strict';

				    var events_to_monitor = [
				        'wc-ajax=get_refreshed_fragments',
				        'wc-ajax=remove_from_cart',
				    ];

				    function handle_mini_cart_ajax_events( settings, show_loading ) {
				        if ( events_to_monitor.some( function( event ) {
				            return settings.url.indexOf( event ) !== -1;
				        })) {
				            if ( show_loading ) {
				                $( '#ajax-loading' ).css( 'display', 'block' );
				            } else {
				                $( '#ajax-loading' ).css( 'display', 'none' );
				            }
				        }
				    }

				    events_to_monitor.forEach( function( event ) {
				        $( document ).ajaxSend( function( event, jqXHR, settings ) {
				            handle_mini_cart_ajax_events( settings, true ); 
				        });
				        $( document ).ajaxComplete( function( event, jqXHR, settings ) {
				            handle_mini_cart_ajax_events( settings, false );
				        });
				    });
				    
				// Close anon function.
				}( jQuery ) );
				
//# sourceURL=shoptimizer-main-js-after
/* ]]> */
</script>
